- **foresight-auditor**: Use to validate the existing regression corpus and interpret coverage results. Runs the deterministic foresight.py audit + coverage, then reviews the findings against the actual code — confirming real gaps, catching the heuristic's false "uncovered"/"weak" matches, and explaining what each broken regression entry needs to become replayable and sound. Read-only on source; writes only its own notes. ([sobryan/foresight](https://github.com/sobryan/foresight/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/foresight-auditor.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **foresight-cartographer**: Use to build the static inventory of what a product does — its features, use cases, UI elements, routes/screens — by reading the code and docs and running read-only shell. Does not edit code, drive the app, or judge coverage. ([sobryan/foresight](https://github.com/sobryan/foresight/blob/HEAD/agents/foresight-cartographer.md) · 2 copies)
- **foresight-mobile-explorer**: Use to explore a running ANDROID or iOS app — walk its screens, capture the UI hierarchy and a screenshot per step, and map elements to use cases. Enriches inventory.json and writes per-run screenshots + ui_map.json. Read-only by default. ([sobryan/foresight](https://github.com/sobryan/foresight/blob/HEAD/agents/foresight-mobile-explorer.md) · 2 copies)
- **foresight-visual-inspector**: Use after an exploration run to look at its screenshots the way a person would — enumerate every visually distinct UI element, write a user story for each, and merge them into inventory.json with screenshot provenance. ([sobryan/foresight](https://github.com/sobryan/foresight/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/foresight-visual-inspector.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **foresight-web-explorer**: Explore a running WEB app through the Playwright MCP server — navigate its routes, exercise primary end-to-end flows, save a screenshot per step, and record what each UI element does. Read-only by default; never performs destructive actions on a real target. - **forgewright-builder**: Forgewright builder tier (balanced cost). Use for normal bounded implementation, refactors, tests, debugging and code review on an independent scope with clear acceptance checks. Do NOT use for security, permission boundaries, schema/public API/export contracts, concurrency/locking, payment/billing/IAP/entitlements, migrations, irreversible release paths or repeated failures — use forgewright-expert. Independent review of a finished change goes to forgewright-reviewer and STANDARD/DEEP planning to forgewright-planner. Use forgewright-scout for read-only lookups. ([buiphucminhtam/forgewright](https://github.com/buiphucminhtam/forgewright/blob/HEAD/agents/forgewright-builder.md) · ★49)
- **forgewright-expert**: Forgewright expert tier (most capable, most expensive). Use only when a HARD signal applies: security or permission boundaries, schema/public API/export contracts, concurrency/locking/ordering, payment/billing/IAP/entitlements/subscription/checkout, migrations or irreversible release paths, disagreeing evidence, or a step that already failed twice. Independent review of a finished change goes to forgewright-reviewer and planning to forgewright-planner. Routine work should use forgewright-builder; lookups should use forgewright-scout. ([buiphucminhtam/forgewright](https://github.com/buiphucminhtam/forgewright/blob/HEAD/agents/forgewright-expert.md) · ★49)
- **forgewright-planner**: Forgewright planner tier (most capable model, planning only). Use for STANDARD/DEEP planning and design decisions: locking a plan (PLAN_LOCKED), task decomposition for parallel or goal-driven work, architecture and tech-stack choices, requirements, roadmaps and sprint plans, game design (GDD, economy, levels, narrative), and open-ended exploration of what to build. It reads and researches but never edits files. QUICK work stays in the parent; implementation goes to forgewright-builder or forgewright-expert. ([buiphucminhtam/forgewright](https://github.com/buiphucminhtam/forgewright/blob/HEAD/agents/forgewright-planner.md) · ★49)
- **forgewright-reviewer**: Forgewright reviewer tier (most capable model, independent review only). Use for independent adversarial review of a finished change, including the keyless review-2 that HARD changes require: it must be a different model from the implementer. Give it requirements, the diff and raw evidence, not the implementer's reasoning. It runs checks but never edits files. Routine code review during implementation can stay with forgewright-builder. ([buiphucminhtam/forgewright](https://github.com/buiphucminhtam/forgewright/blob/HEAD/agents/forgewright-reviewer.md) · ★49)
- **forgewright-scout**: Forgewright scout tier (cheapest). Use for bounded read-only or mechanical work whose answer is checkable: locating files/symbols, inventories, status extraction, summarizing files, logs or diffs already on disk. It has no shell. Do NOT use for running commands, edits, design decisions, security, schema, public API, concurrency, payment/billing, migrations or releases — use forgewright-builder or forgewright-expert instead.
